- [NOTE] 2026-10-03 Avid EX 24.1 sensor-channel reconciliation from the official CRP5210-01E connector-and-pin PDF: the 4-pin M12 drawing is a female-face view (male numbering mirrors). J7.1 supplies +24 V to multiple M12 pin 1 contacts; J7.2 supplies sensor GND to M12 pin 3; J7.3 takes X home/limit from M12 pin 4; J7.5 takes Y1 home/limit; J7.7 takes Y2 home; J7.11 takes A home; J7.13 takes Y+ limit; J7.14 takes E-stop; J7.15 takes Z+ home/limit. The Acorn-side destinations are J8.1 E-stop IN1, J8.2 grouped X/Y1/Z/Y+ IN2, J8.3 grouped Y2/A IN3, J8.4 auto Z touchplate IN4. These source assignments do not validate direct connection to SmoothieBox: retain the full M12 connector orientation and rated field-side supply, remove/mark the old Avid-to-Acorn path OPEN when repurposing it, and show any sensor-to-SmoothieBox channel as GUESS through a selected, rated isolation/conditioning interface until sensor type/output and controller logic levels are verified. Do not collapse Y2/A onto ZMIN or imply six axis-specific destination signals are source-confirmed. Sources: [Avid EX 24.1 schematics page](https://www.avidcnc.com/support/instructions/electronics/ex/manual/stepper/24.1/schematics/) and [CRP5210-01E interconnect-board PDF](https://www.avidcnc.com/support/instructions/electronics/ex/manual/pdf/schematics/24.1/Avid_CNC_EX_Interconnect_Board_24.1.PDF), especially PDF pages 4-5. This is source reconciliation only; no production graph, SVG, or article was changed in this step.

- [NOTE] 2026-10-03 Avid axis-function mapping correction: the CRP5210-01E EX 24.1 source does **not** establish six separate X−, X+, Y−, Y+, Z+, and Y2 host destinations. It specifies X Home/Limit (J7.3), Y1 Home/Limit (J7.5), Y2 Home (J7.7), A Home (J7.11), Y+ Limit (J7.13), Z+ Home/Limit (J7.15), plus Emergency Stop (J7.14); J8 groups those into Acorn IN2/IN3 channels. The current Avid guide’s six separate axis-side M12 labels and SmoothieBox XMIN/XMAX/YMIN/YMAX/ZMAX/ZMIN proposal are therefore unverified semantics even when the source pin schedule is correct. Preserve the exact physical M12/socket and sourced J7 names; label any reassignment to SmoothieBox as GUESS and OPEN until machine-specific harness tracing, sensor identity, and intended firmware homing/limit behavior are established. In particular, do not move the A-home or Y2-home signals into ZMIN by assumption. Source: official [CRP5210-01E 24.1 interconnect-board PDF](https://www.avidcnc.com/support/instructions/electronics/ex/manual/pdf/schematics/24.1/Avid_CNC_EX_Interconnect_Board_24.1.PDF), pp. 4-5. This is an evidence correction only; no production graph, article, or SVG changed.

- [NOTE] 2026-10-03 Avid M12 contact-population/version boundary: the EX 24.1 CRP5210-01E PDF sources M12 pin 1 (+24 V), pin 3 (GND), and pin 4 (signal), and specifies that its common M12 illustration is a female-face view mirrored on male connectors; it does not state whether pin 2 is physically fitted on every associated CRP860 sensor socket. Older CRP860-00E manuals (e.g. [Avid CNC 21.2 schematics](https://www.avidcnc.com/support/instructions/electronics/pnp/manual/21.2/schematics/)) say sensor/Aux pin 2 (Normally Closed) is not populated and E-stop pin 4 (Normally Open) is not populated. Do not project that older board’s per-socket population onto the EX 24.1 harness without revision-specific evidence. In each final Avid peripheral drawing, show the complete four-contact A-coded M12 face and mark pin 2 NC / E-stop pin 4 status as revision-conditional until the exact fitted CRP860 port is confirmed; retain correct female/male viewing orientation.

- [NOTE] 2026-10-03 Avid sensor-input voltage caution: an official 2025Q1.1 Plug & Play/CRP860-00E manual describes the custom M12 inputs as normally-open and rated for 12 V, while the separate EX 24.1 CRP5210-01E interconnect schedule supplies +24 V to M12 pin 1. These documents describe different controller revisions; neither establishes the actual input front-end rating, sensor model, or output topology of the specific Avid EX machine. Do not feed a potentially 24 V M12 output into a Smoothie input or assume that a 12 V CRP860 input rating transfers to the EX harness. Keep the sensor signal and host route OPEN pending fitted-board/sensor verification; show a rated isolation/level-conditioning interface as GUESS, not as approved hardware. Source: [Avid CNC 22.2/2025Q1.1 initial setup](https://www.avidcnc.com/support/instructions/electronics/pnp/manual/22.2/initialSetup/) and [EX 24.1 CRP5210-01E PDF](https://www.avidcnc.com/support/instructions/electronics/ex/manual/pdf/schematics/24.1/Avid_CNC_EX_Interconnect_Board_24.1.PDF).

- [NOTE] 2026-10-03 Avid spindle 0–10 V adapter candidate, now source-qualified at the module-pin level: DFRobot DFR1036 / GP8101S is a real one-channel PWM-to-0–5/0–10 V board. Its product guide specifies 3.3–5 V module supply, PWM D/GND/VCC input, selectable 0–5/0–10 V output and VOUT/GND terminals. The GP8101S datasheet specifies PWM high level 2.7–5.5 V, 50 Hz–50 kHz frequency, and 20 mA output at VOUT >0.5 V; it requires a 9–36 V IC rail, while DFR1036’s official schematic shows an onboard MT3608 boost from its 3.3–5 V host supply to 12 V. The board schematic uses the same GND net at host input and analog output: it is not galvanically isolated. Proposed 0–10 V branch: SmoothieBox Laser PWM output + GND → DFR1036 D + VCC/GND (verify fitted PWM HIGH level/frequency and safe host 5 V source) → select 0–10 V → DFR1036 VOUT/GND → VFD analog input/analog common (verify VFD input impedance and whether common may bond to controller ground). This adapter does not supply spindle RUN/FWD: draw a separate verified relay/isolated contact path to the VFD’s RUN/DCM contacts. Treat all new interconnects as dotted GUESS until exact fitted VFD pins, analog common, firmware PWM rate/polarity and host supply are confirmed. The Avid 0–10 V figure must not be shown as a direct PWM-to-AI wire, and the board must not be labelled isolated. Sources: [DFRobot DFR1036 guide](https://wiki.dfrobot.com/dfr1036/), [DFRobot DFR1036 schematic PDF](https://dfimg.dfrobot.com/wiki/18622/DFR1036_gp8101s-dac_schematics_1.0.pdf), and [GP8101S datasheet PDF](https://dfimg.dfrobot.com/60c1e008bddfc41c3293de80/wiki/b86e46b39945acb8bcfe6429bef0df15.pdf).

- [NOTE] 2026-10-03 J35.6-to-DAC threshold check: the GP8101S specifies PWM VIH ≥2.7 V, but ST STM32F405/407 Table 49 has different guaranteed VOH minima for CMOS vs TTL ports (and load conditions). SmoothieBox source currently establishes the MCU pad name PE9, not the fitted silicon/revision, I/O mode, local supply, firmware PWM settings or loaded waveform. Thus DFR1036 is a plausible adapter candidate, not a proven directly compatible/safe route; retain dotted GUESS until fitted hardware/waveform under load is verified. Source links and interpretation are in `docs/machine-wiring-design-rules.md`.

- [NOTE] 2026-10-03 Avid pin-number correction from manufacturer sources: the 14-pin Avid SP/THC **control cable** assigns pin 1 Fault Ground, 2 Fault Signal, 7 FWD, 8 DCM, 9 AVI, 10 ACM, and optional 11 10V Ref. These are connector pins, not VFD screw-terminal numbers. The official Avid 3 HP CRP450-00E-01 schematic is scoped to revisions 18.4/20.1 and VFD ID A1; its drive-side controls are function-labelled (DCM/AFI/ACM/+10V/AVI/MI1–MI6). The prior note's `1/2 ready/fault` and “VFD terminals 7–10” wording was inaccurate: correct status is Fault Ground/Signal on Avid cable pins 1/2, FWD/DCM on pins 7/8 and AVI/ACM on pins 9/10. Do not copy those numbers onto unidentified VFD terminals. Whether the exact `mill-avid-ex-3` owns this cable/system revision still requires machine-article evidence; keep the VFD side OPEN until reconciled. Sources: Avid schematics https://www.avidcnc.com/support/instructions/accessories/spindles/setup/technical/schematics/ ; Avid 3HP schematic https://www.avidcnc.com/dl/cad.pdf/CRP450-00E-01-20.1-A_20220217.pdf ; system identification https://www.avidcnc.com/support/instructions/accessories/spindles/setup/technical/systemIdentification/ .
